=== BUGGY CODE ===
class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for i, j in edges:
            g[i].append(j)
            g[j].append(i)
            
        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n
        
        def dfs(i, l, p:
            level[i] = l
            parent[i] = p
            for j in g[i]:
                if j != p:
                    dfs(j, l + 1, i)
        
        undefinedLCA(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a
        
        dfs(0, 0, -1)
        for i, j in trips:
            lca = LCA(i, j)
            path = []
            while i != lca:
                freq[i] += 1
                i = parent[i]
            freq[i] += 1
            while j != lca:
                freq[j] += 1
                j = parent[j]
        
        def dp(i, p):
            res0 = 0
            res1 = price[i] // 2 * freq[i]
            for j in g[i]:
                if j != p:
                    curr = dp(j, i)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]
        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))

=== PROMPT ===
Observe the following faulty python code. It contains one or more bugs.
Fix the code and output only the fixed code.

```python
class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for i, j in edges:
            g[i].append(j)
            g[j].append(i)
            
        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n
        
        def dfs(i, l, p:
            level[i] = l
            parent[i] = p
            for j in g[i]:
                if j != p:
                    dfs(j, l + 1, i)
        
        undefinedLCA(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a
        
        dfs(0, 0, -1)
        for i, j in trips:
            lca = LCA(i, j)
            path = []
            while i != lca:
                freq[i] += 1
                i = parent[i]
            freq[i] += 1
            while j != lca:
                freq[j] += 1
                j = parent[j]
        
        def dp(i, p):
            res0 = 0
            res1 = price[i] // 2 * freq[i]
            for j in g[i]:
                if j != p:
                    curr = dp(j, i)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]
        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))
```

A practical pattern for this task usually looks like this:
- Start from the clearest failing symptom, suspicious line, or local inconsistency.
- Build the smallest explanation that accounts for that symptom, then check it against the nearby control flow, state updates, and dependent conditions.
- Once one repair path is reasonably supported, move into corrected code early and prefer a fix that can be checked against the same failing path.
- After the edit, rerun that path or the most relevant edge case.
- Then do one small coupled-region scan: a directly connected guard, index update, return path, or call site that could still break the same fix.
- If the recheck is still off, refine the same local region before opening a distant new theory.
- Once the failing path and its directly coupled region look consistent, finalize the code.

Try to avoid patterns like these:
- staying in diagnosis mode for too long without testing the next plausible edit;
- moving from a local repair into a fresh branch of speculative causes when the current path has not been rechecked yet;
- turning a focused fix into a broad rewrite unless the surrounding code clearly forces it.

```
    not_halve = price[node] * cnt[node]
    halve = price[node] * cnt[node] // 2
    for child in graph[node]:

        halve += child_not_halve

return min(dfs(0,-1))
```

```
def dfs(u, p):
    a = price[u] * cnt[u]
    b = price[u] * cnt[u] // 2
    for v in g[u]:
        if v == p: continue
        x, y = dfs(v, u)
        a += min(x, y)
        b += x
    return a, b
return min(dfs(0, -1))
```

```
class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for a,b in edges:
            g[a].append(b); g[b].append(a)
        cnt = [0]*n
        for a,b in trips:
            # find path...
        def dfs(u,p):
            a = price[u]*cnt[u]
            b = price[u]*cnt[u]//2
            for v in g[u]:
                if v==p: continue
                x,y = dfs(v,u)
                a += min(x,y)
                b += x
            return a,b
        return min(dfs(0,-1))
```

Let's ensure code syntax:

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for i, j in edges:
            g[i].append(j)
            g[j].append(i)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(i, l, p):
            level[i] = l
            parent[i] = p
            for j in g[i]:
                if j != p:
                    dfs(j, l + 1, i)

        def LCA(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for start, end in trips:
            lca = LCA(start, end)
            x, y = start, end
            while x != lca:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[lca] += 1
            while y != lca:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(i, p):
            not_half = price[i] * freq[i]
            half = price[i] * freq[i] // 2
            for j in g[i]:
                if j != p:
                    child_not, child_half = dp(j, i)
                    not_half += min(child_not, child_half)
                    half += child_not
            return [not_half, half]

        return min(dp(0, -1))
```

Need check if `parent[0] = -1`, and in LCA while `a != b`, if a or b becomes -1? Suppose a and b are in different components? no. Suppose one is root and other not, after raising, a==b root, return. Good.

Potential issue: In trip loop, if `start` and `end` are same, lca=start, x=start, first while skipped, freq[start]+=1, second while skipped. Good.

Potential issue: In DP, if root has parent -1, for child j != -1 all children. Good.

Potential issue: The original code's `undefinedLCA` was not defined; we fixed.

Now, let's think about whether the DP should be min cost or max saving. Direct min cost is standard. But if we use direct min cost, we don't need `ans`. Good.

[1,2]: LCA(1,2): levels equal? level1=1, level2=2, raise 2 to 1: b=1. a==b=1. x=1, y=2. while x !=1 skip; freq[1]+=1. while y!=1: freq[2]+=1,y=1. freq [1,2,2,1]. Good.

```
    res0 = 0
    res1 = price[i]*freq[i] - price[i]*freq[i]//2
    for j...
       curr=dp(j,i)
       res0 += max(curr)
       res1 += curr[0]
    return [res0,res1]
return ans - max(dp(0,-1))
```
This also works. But direct min is simpler and less risk. Need ensure direct min uses `half = price[i] * freq[i] // 2` not `price[i] // 2 * freq[i]`. We'll use direct.

Let's write final code cleanly:

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u, l, p):
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(u, v):
            if level[u] < level[v]:
                u, v = v, u
            diff = level[u] - level[v]
            while diff:
                u = parent[u]
                diff -= 1
            if u == v:
                return u
            while u != v:
                u = parent[u]
                v = parent[v]
            return u

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for u, v in trips:
            w = lca(u, v)
            x, y = u, v
            while x != w:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[w] += 1
            while y != w:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(u, p):
            not_half = price[u] * freq[u]
            half = price[u] * freq[u] // 2
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    child_not, child_half = dp(v, u)
                    not_half += min(child_not, child_half)
                    half += child_not
            return [not_half, half]

        return min(dp(0, -1))
```

```
while d:
    b = parent[b]
```

```
    u, v = v, u

while diff:

```

```
private int[] dfs(int node, int parent) {
```

Let's maybe search memory of LeetCode 2646 "minimumTotalPrice" official solution in Python:
```
class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for a,b in edges:
            graph[a].append(b)
            graph[b].append(a)
        count = [0]*n
        for start,end in trips:
            # find path via dfs
            ...
        def dfs(node,parent):
            not_half = price[node] * count[node]
            half = price[node] * count[node] // 2
            for child in graph[node]:
                if child == parent: continue
                child_not, child_half = dfs(child,node)
                not_half += min(child_not, child_half)
                half += child_not
            return not_half, half
        return min(dfs(0,-1))
```
Yes.

=== RAW ANSWER ===
```python
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u, l, p):
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(u, v):
            if level[u] < level[v]:
                u, v = v, u
            diff = level[u] - level[v]
            while diff:
                u = parent[u]
                diff -= 1
            if u == v:
                return u
            while u != v:
                u = parent[u]
                v = parent[v]
            return u

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for u, v in trips:
            w = lca(u, v)
            x, y = u, v
            while x != w:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[w] += 1
            while y != w:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(u, p):
            not_half = price[u] * freq[u]
            half = price[u] * freq[u] // 2

            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    child_not, child_half = dp(v, u)
                    not_half += min(child_not, child_half)
                    half += child_not

            return [not_half, half]

        return min(dp(0, -1))
```

=== FIXED CODE ===
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u, l, p):
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(u, v):
            if level[u] < level[v]:
                u, v = v, u
            diff = level[u] - level[v]
            while diff:
                u = parent[u]
                diff -= 1
            if u == v:
                return u
            while u != v:
                u = parent[u]
                v = parent[v]
            return u

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for u, v in trips:
            w = lca(u, v)
            x, y = u, v
            while x != w:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[w] += 1
            while y != w:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(u, p):
            not_half = price[u] * freq[u]
            half = price[u] * freq[u] // 2

            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    child_not, child_half = dp(v, u)
                    not_half += min(child_not, child_half)
                    half += child_not

            return [not_half, half]

        return min(dp(0, -1))
